Ruling: model error; the candidate needs answers, applies screens or shows amounts on completed returns where the source does not require them. Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/dependent.deepseek.json): 5464 of 9433 comparisons agree; on completed returns {"candidate-shows-skipped": 11, "candidate-unknown": 1217}.

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| dependent.claimed | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support | - |
| dependent.ctc_child | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support | dependent.multiple_support |
| dependent.odc_person | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support | - |
| dependent.ctc_amount | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support | dependent.multiple_support |
| dependent.odc_amount | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support | - |
| dependent.actc_amount | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support | dependent.multiple_support |
| dependent.hoh_test2 | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support | - |
| dependent.hoh_test1 | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support | - |
| dependent.eic_child | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support |
| dependent.eic_ssn_child | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support |
| dependent.dc_qp | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support | - |
| dependent.dc_count | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support |
| 27 | dependent.edu_over | dependent.edu_over |
| 28 | dependent.edu_over | dependent.edu_over |

| 31 | dependent.edu_over | dependent.edu_over |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: dependent.dc_line2d
    printed: "2(d)"
    label: "Qualified expenses you incurred and paid in 2025 for the person listed in column (a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: dependent.dc_paid}
  - id: dependent.dc_line2c
    printed: "2(c)"
    label: "Check here if the qualifying person was over age 12 and was disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: dependent.dc_over12_care}
  - id: dependent.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (American opportunity credit), not more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [dependent.h_edu_adj_aoc_raw, dependent.h_aoc_cap]}
    refuses: [dependent.edu_over]
  - id: dependent.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.edu_line27, take: {const: 200000}}
  - id: dependent.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: dependent.edu_line28, rate: {param: aoc.second_rate}}
  - id: dependent.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}
          then: dependent.edu_line27
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}}
          then: dependent.h_line30_add
  - id: dependent.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (lifetime learning credit)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.edu_inst, take: dependent.h_edu_adj_sum}
    refuses: [dependent.edu_over]
  - id: dependent.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One dependent entry"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: dependent.one}
  - id: dependent.claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is claimed as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr, {const: true}]}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_citizen_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule]
  - id: dependent.ctc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent is a qualifying child for the child tax credit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: ctc.age_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.work_ssn, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.odc_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent qualifies for the credit for other dependents"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}}
  - id: dependent.ctc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Child tax credit amount for this dependent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_ctc_per_child
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}}
          then: dependent.h_zero_money
  - id: dependent.odc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit for other dependents amount for this dependent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_odc_per_dependent
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}}
          then: dependent.h_zero_money
  - id: dependent.actc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional child tax credit child amount for this dependent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_actc_per_child
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}}
          then: dependent.h_zero_money
  - id: dependent.hoh_absence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "An absence counted for this person makes the head of household time invalid"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: dependent.absence_no_return}
  - id: dependent.hoh_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is a qualifying person under head of household Test 2"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.home_cost, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_res_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.absence_no_return, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_hoh_excluded, {const: true}]}}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: false}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.hoh_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is a parent qualifying person under head of household Test 1"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.parent_home_paid, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.multiple_support, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.eic_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person meets the relationship, age, residence and joint-return tests to be a qualifying child for the EIC"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_joint_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_other_taxpayer_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.eic_ssn_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is an EIC qualifying child with a valid SSN for the EIC"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.dc_qp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is a qualifying person for child and dependent care expenses"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_dc_child, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_dc_disabled, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.dc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying persons for child and dependent care expenses"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.ptc_magi_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent's modified AGI counted for the premium tax credit"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.ptc_magi
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}}
          then: dependent.h_zero_money
  - id: dependent.h_zero_money
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero dollars"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: dependent.h_ctc_per_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Child tax credit per qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ctc.per_child}
  - id: dependent.h_odc_per_dependent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit for other dependents per dependent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: odc.per_dependent}
  - id: dependent.h_actc_per_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional child tax credit per qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: actc.per_child}
  - id: dependent.h_aoc_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "American opportunity credit expense cap"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.expense_cap}
  - id: dependent.h_aoc_full_band
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "American opportunity credit full 100% expense band"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.full_band}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_adj_sum
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total education expense adjustments"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_a2025, dependent.edu_a2026, dependent.edu_refunds]}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_total_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total American opportunity credit qualified expenses before adjustments"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_inst, dependent.edu_mat]}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_adj_aoc_raw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "American opportunity credit adjusted expenses before the $4,000 cap"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.h_edu_total_aoc, take: dependent.h_edu_adj_sum}
  - id: dependent.h_line30_add
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8863 line 29 plus $2,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_line29, dependent.h_aoc_full_band]}
  - id: dependent.h_rel_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Relationship test for a qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}
  - id: dependent.h_age_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Age test for a qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.student_age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.student, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.disabled, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_res_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Residence test for a qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [dependent.months, {const: 6, type: Count}]}
  - id: dependent.h_joint_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Joint-return test for a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.h_self_support_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Support test for a qualifying child (did not provide over half of own support)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [dependent.self_support, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.h_other_taxpayer_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Person is not the qualifying child of another taxpayer"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      not:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_nontaxpayer, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.h_citizen_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Citizen, national, resident alien or Canada/Mexico resident test"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.canada_mexico, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person meets the qualifying child tests"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_self_support_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_joint_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_res_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_other_taxpayer_ok, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_rel_qr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Member of household or relationship test for a qualifying relative"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent_relative"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "in_law"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "household_member"}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "other"}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qr_all_year, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.lawful, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_gross_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Gross income test for a qualifying relative"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]}
  - id: dependent.h_support_qr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Support test for a qualifying relative"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [dependent.supported, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person meets the qualifying relative tests"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qr, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_other_taxpayer_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_gross_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_support_qr, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_joint_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_citizen_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}}
    refuses: [dependent.multiple_support]
  - id: dependent.h_age_under13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person was under age 13 while care was provided"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_dc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child under age 13 for child and dependent care expenses"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_under13, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_dc_disabled
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Disabled qualifying person for child and dependent care expenses"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_res_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_hoh_excluded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent is excluded from head of household Test 2"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.divorced_rule, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "household_member"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "other"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.multiple_support, {const: true}]}
```
